# WASB Reimplementation + Golden Eval — Investigation Report

**Critical framing finding up front:** This repo's F1/tolF1 harness scores **rally time-boundary intervals** (start/end seconds), NOT per-pixel shuttle position. There is **no per-frame shuttle-position F1 (TrackNet-style within-τ-pixels) anywhere in this repo.** The WASB detector emits a per-frame trajectory CSV; that feeds rally windowing; F1/tolF1 are measured on the resulting rally intervals. If you want a per-pixel shuttle metric to validate your reimplementation, you'll have to build it (or diff trajectory CSVs directly). See §2.

Also: the WASB-SBDT model repo (`~/models/WASB-SBDT`) is an **external MIT dependency, not cloned locally** (`C:/Users/avidu/models/WASB-SBDT` is empty). The repo-side caller (`wasb_infer.py`) reads all model dims from Hydra config at runtime, so exact normalization constants live upstream. Where I can't confirm from local code, I inspected the actual weights file and flag the rest as open.

---

## 1. WASB inference internals

All in `backend/pipeline/detectors/wasb_infer.py`. The runner `native_wasb_runner.py` is just a subprocess wrapper that stages this file into `~/models/WASB-SBDT/src/` and invokes it.

### Model construction + weight load
- Config is composed via **Hydra** (`_build_cfg`, `wasb_infer.py:57-78`):
  ```python
  compose(config_name="eval", overrides=[
      f"dataset={sport}",           # badminton
      "model=wasb",                 # -&gt; name=hrnet
      f"detector.model_path={weights}",
      f"runner.device={device}",    # cuda default
      f"runner.gpus={gpus}",        # [0] on cuda
  ])
  ```
- The model is built by **WASB's own `build_detector(cfg)`** (`wasb_infer.py:499, 601`) — HRNet construction and the `torch.load`/`load_state_dict` happen **inside WASB's `detectors/detector.py::TracknetV2Detector`**, which is NOT in this repo (confirmed `docs/CODE_MAP.md:269-275`). So you must replicate HRNet + the load from the upstream repo.
- **Weight file structure — verified directly** from `scratch/stage/wasb_badminton_best.pth.tar`:
  - `torch.load(...)` returns a dict with a **single key: `model_state_dict`** (428 entries).
  - So the load is `model.load_state_dict(torch.load(path, map_location=...)["model_state_dict"])`.
  - First keys: `conv1.weight, bn1.{weight,bias,running_mean,running_var,num_batches_tracked}, conv2…` — a standard HRNet stem.
  - **`conv1.weight` shape = `(64, 9, 3, 3)`** → **9 input channels = 3 frames × 3 RGB** stacked on the channel axis.
  - **`final_layers.0.weight` shape = `(3, 16, 1, 1)`, `final_layers.0.bias` = `(3,)`** → **3 output heatmaps** (one per input frame; `frames_out=3`), 1×1 conv head from 16 feature channels.

### Preprocessing (the exact numbers you need)
Read from `cfg["model"]` at runtime (`wasb_infer.py:504-506`):
```python
frames_in  = int(cfg["model"]["frames_in"])                                    # = 3
input_wh   = (cfg["model"]["inp_width"], cfg["model"]["inp_height"])           # = (512, 288)  W×H
output_wh  = (cfg["model"]["out_width"], cfg["model"]["out_height"])           # = (512, 288)
```
- **Input resolution: 512×288 (W×H)** — confirmed `docs/CODE_MAP.md:271`: "`frames_in=3, frames_out=3, input 512x288 (WxH), ImageNet normalize`".
- **Normalization: ImageNet mean/std** (`CODE_MAP.md:271`). The exact constants (`[0.485,0.456,0.406]`/`[0.229,0.224,0.225]`) are applied inside WASB's `build_img_transforms(cfg)` (`wasb_infer.py:487, 581`) — **not in this repo; confirm the exact values + whether a `/255` scale precedes it from upstream `dataloaders`.** ⚠ open.
- **Color order: RGB.** The disk path is `Image.open(path).convert('RGB')` (WASB's `read_image`, `wasb_infer.py:370-373`); the streaming path deliberately reproduces it byte-for-byte via `cv2.cvtColor(bgr, COLOR_BGR2RGB)` → PIL RGB (`_bgr_to_pil_rgb`, `wasb_infer.py:328-340`).
- **Windowing / stacking:** sliding windows of **3 consecutive frames**, stride 1 (`build_windows`, `wasb_infer.py:257-266`): `[frames[i:i+3] for i in range(N-2)]`. Each window → sample `{"frames":[3 paths], "annos":[Center(is_visible=False,x=-1,y=-1) per frame]}` (`wasb_infer.py:529-535`). The 3 frames are concatenated on the channel dim → 9-channel input inside WASB's `ImageDataset` (`wasb_infer.py:586-594`). One window = one unit of GPU work.

### Forward + postprocess
- Forward: **`detector.run_tensor(imgs, trans) -&gt; (batch_results, hms_vis)`** (`wasb_infer.py:646`). `trans` = the affine transform matrices that map heatmap coords back to original-frame pixels. The heatmap-decode (argmax/blob/threshold) happens **inside WASB's `run_tensor`** (upstream, not in repo) — `CODE_MAP.md:272` describes the contract: `results[bid][eid]` = list of candidate dicts **`{xy: np.array (ORIGINAL image coords), score, scale}`**. ⚠ The actual decode algorithm (it's a blob/connected-component detector with a score threshold and multi-scale in WASB, not a plain argmax) must be read from upstream `detectors/detector.py`.
- Per-frame candidate dicts are accumulated by frame id across all windows the frame appears in (`det_by_fid`, `wasb_infer.py:648-656`) and cached to `det_raw.jsonl` as `[x,y,score,scale]` (`_det_plain`, `wasb_infer.py:112-115`).
- **Trajectory from candidates:** WASB's **stateful `OnlineTracker`** (`build_tracker(cfg)`, `wasb_infer.py:685-690`; `CODE_MAP.md:273`). `tracker.refresh()` then `tracker.update(frame_dets)` per frame **in strict order** → `{x, y, visi, score}`. It does `np.linalg.norm` vector math, so `xy` must be a real numpy array (`_dets_to_tracker`, `wasb_infer.py:118-126`). Deterministic on a full ordered re-run. The tracker logic is also upstream. ⚠

### Output format
`_atomic_write_trajectory` (`wasb_infer.py:228-237`, rows built at `687-692`) emits a **CSV with header `Frame,Visibility,X,Y`**, one row per frame:
- `Frame` = integer frame id; `Visibility` = `1 if tracker says visible else 0`; `X,Y` = tracked shuttle pixel coords in **original-frame** resolution (or `-1,-1` when not visible).
- This is exactly the shape `TrackNetRunner.parse_trajectory_csv` consumes (model-agnostic; shared with TrackNet).

---

## 2. Golden eval harness — how F1/tolF1 is computed

**There are two interval metrics; both are rally-time-boundary, τ is in SECONDS, not pixels.**

**(A) tIoU-F1 — the current gate.** `run_eval.py` → `backend/eval/harness.py`:
- A prediction matches a GT rally when **temporal IoU (overlap/union) ≥ threshold**, greedy 1:1. Default **IoU τ = 0.5** (`run_eval.py:73`). P=TP/(TP+FP), R=TP/(TP+FN), F1=harmonic mean (`docs/EVALUATION.md`).
- Scores predictions **already in the SQLite DB** — it does NOT run the pipeline.

**(B) tolF1 / F1@τ — the R2 tolerance metric** (`backend/eval/tolerance_metrics.py`, reported alongside tIoU by `rally_seg_eval.py`):
- A prediction matches a golden rally iff **`|Δstart| ≤ τ_start` AND `|Δend| ≤ τ_end`** under a **strict 1:1 Hungarian assignment** (scipy `linear_sum_assignment`; greedy fallback if no scipy) — `tolerance_metrics.py:34-119`.
- **τ values (seconds): `TAU_START = 2.0`, `TAU_END = 1.5`** (`tolerance_metrics.py:29-31`); symmetric sweep `τ ∈ {1.5, 2.0, 2.5, 3.0}` (`SWEEP_TAUS`, line 31). Asymmetric because the service window makes rally-*start* fuzzy. These are provisional pending an IAA study (`docs/R2_EVAL_METRIC_DESIGN.md`).
- `tol_f1 = matched·2·P·R/(P+R)`, `tol_precision = matched/n_pred`, `tol_recall = matched/n_gt` (`tolerance_metrics.py:98-119`, emitted by `rally_seg_eval.score_intervals` at `140-142`). Relationship: `tolF1@~3.0s ≈ tIoU-F1@0.5`.

**"tolF1 vs F1":** F1 (a.k.a. `f1@0.25`/`f1@0.5`) = overlap-IoU-gated match at that IoU. tolF1 = boundary-tolerance-gated match within τ seconds. Both are rally-level.

### Commands
- **DB-based single video (tIoU-F1):**
  ```
  python run_eval.py --video "C:/path/match.mp4" --annotations rallies.csv --stage both --iou 0.5
  # or: python run_eval.py --video-id &lt;md5&gt; --annotations rallies.csv --iou 0.5
  ```
  Needs: the video **already processed** into `output/sports_indexer.db` + the GT `rallies.csv`.
- **Trajectory-CSV based, full golden corpus (both f1@k and tol_f1, no DB, no GPU):** this is the one that actually exercises the WASB output end-to-end:
  ```
  python -m backend.eval.rally_seg_eval --manifest output/human_lovo_manifest.json --features-dir output --iou 0.5
  ```
  `rally_seg_eval.evaluate` (`rally_seg_eval.py:312`) loops the manifest, re-windows each trajectory CSV, and scores vs `&lt;name&gt;_golden_features.json` `gts`. There is **no per-video `--only`** here; it scores the whole manifest. Optional `--tau-start/--tau-end` override the R2 τ.
- **Per-video rally calibration from a single trajectory CSV** (closest to a single-clip numbers run):
  ```
  python -m backend.eval.calibrate_wasb --trajectory full_traj.csv --labels "X.rallies.csv" --fps 59.94 --frame-width 1920
  ```
  (`calibrate_wasb.py:131-143`)

## 3. Golden video + ground-truth label locations &amp; format

**Manifest (source of truth for pairing):** `output/human_lovo_manifest.json` — a 15-entry JSON list, each entry `{name, trajectory, fps, frame_width, labels}`. GCS mirror: `gs://khelsutra-rally-corpus/manifests/golden.json`.

**Videos:**
- GCS contract layout: `gs://khelsutra-rally-corpus/videos/&lt;name&gt;.mp4`, `.../labels/&lt;date&gt;_&lt;name&gt;.rallies.csv`, `.../trajectories/&lt;name&gt;.csv` (`docs/DATA_IN_GCS.md`). **⚠ Reality: only 1/15 videos actually migrated** (`mahadevpura_1`); the rest live locally on Drive `G:\My Drive\...\Golden Label Videos Request - June 15\` and the `F:\[Khelsutra] GoPro...` archive.
- The trajectory CSVs (the GPU-free eval input — you don't need the raw videos to run `rally_seg_eval`) are referenced in the manifest at `C:/Users/avidu/Projects/Annotation Setup/Collect/Trajectories/&lt;...&gt;.csv`.

**Labels (ground truth):**
- Format: `.rallies.csv` with columns **`rally_number,start_time,end_time,ending_reason,sport,shots_count`** (decimal seconds). Verified sample (`scratch/stage/GX010128.rallies.csv`):
  ```
  rally_number,start_time,end_time,ending_reason,sport,shots_count
  1,9.661,32.173,forced_error,badminton,
  ```
- The canonical loader `backend/eval/gt_loader.py` reads **only start/end** (accepts `start`/`start_time` + `end`/`end_time` headers, or positional cols 0,1; ignores all other columns). It does **not** contain per-frame shuttle x,y — these are rally intervals, confirming there is no per-pixel GT here.
- **Pairing:** by `name` — `videos/&lt;name&gt;.mp4` ↔ `labels/&lt;name&gt;.rallies.csv` ↔ `trajectories/&lt;name&gt;.csv`, all joined via the manifest `name`. GCS labels carry a `YYYY-MM-DD_` prefix that's stripped at join time. Strong key is the file MD5 (`backend/utils/hashing.py::compute_video_id`).

**Quick-run subset (2-3 clips), all 59.94 fps, frame_width 1920:**
| name | fps | rallies | video_id | notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `GX010128` | 59.94 | 52 | `db84f3e65318` | has a worked tolF1 preview |
| `mahadevpura_2` | 59.94 | 40 | `038e0e0915a2` | primary R2 preview clip |
| `mahadevpura_1` | 59.94 | 10 | `38bef75d78cb` | smallest; the only clip already in GCS `videos/` |

⚠ **Per-clip pixel resolution is not documented** (only spec target 1080p). `ffprobe` the actual proxy if you need exact WxH. The label `.rallies.csv` files for these are staged locally in `scratch/stage/` (e.g. `GX010128.rallies.csv`, `mahadevpura-2_proxy.rallies.csv`) and mirrored in `output/`.

## 4. Baseline numbers to compare against

`eval_baselines/nightly_baseline.json` (generated 2026-07-03, git_commit `a68f02e`, `iou=0.5`, `manifest=output/human_lovo_manifest.json`). It carries three presets. **The `default` (= `milestone`) preset aggregate is your target:**

| preset | f1@0.25 | f1@0.5 | tol_f1 | tol_precision | tol_recall |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `default` / `milestone` | **0.6351** | **0.4723** | **0.3636** | 0.3194 | 0.4407 |
| `baseline_off` | 0.3998 | 0.2366 | 0.1382 | 0.1454 | 0.1345 |

Per-video `default`-preset numbers are in the same file (`configs.default.per_video.&lt;name&gt;.{f1@0.25,f1@0.5,tol_f1}`), e.g. sanity-check anchors:
- `GX010137`: f1@0.5 = 0.800, tol_f1 = 0.600 (best clip)
- `GX010128`: f1@0.5 = 0.582, tol_f1 = 0.478
- `adarsh_avi_singles`: f1@0.5 = 0.632 (baseline_off), and default per-video available
- Several clips (`mahadevpura_1/2`, `kushagra_singles`) score 0.0 at baseline_off.

Regression gate tolerances (how far a metric may drop before it fails) are separate: `tolerances = {metric_tol: 0.005, over_seg_tol: 0, rate_tol: 0.01}`.

**Important caveat for your reimplementation:** these baselines score the **rally-windowed output**, not raw shuttle detections. They confound (a) detector accuracy with (b) the windowing preset. To validate a torch-2.x WASB reimplementation *specifically*, the cleaner check is: run both the reference `wasb_infer.py` and your reimplementation on the same clip and **diff the `Frame,Visibility,X,Y` trajectory CSVs directly** (sub-pixel float tolerance) — this is exactly the parity gate `native_wasb_runner.py:26-34` documents (validated 2026-06-20: 1194/1195 frames byte-identical on a fixed GPU, lone diff ~5e-5 px). Feeding both CSVs through `rally_seg_eval` and comparing the F1 table is the downstream sanity check.

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
exp_t2.py -- torch-2.x GPU-resident-decode spike for the WASB shuttle detector.

Runs on a Linux L4 GPU VM. Assumes file layout under ~/t2:
    ~/t2/WASB-SBDT          (cloned repo; src/ on this path is importable)
    ~/t2/wasb.pth.tar       (weights: {"model_state_dict": &lt;428-tensor state_dict&gt;})
    ~/t2/g1.mp4             (golden clip, 1080p ~59.94 fps)
    ~/t2/g1_traj_baseline.csv  (cached baseline trajectory; columns Frame,Visibility,X,Y)

Answers:
  Q1 -- can we build WASB HRNet under torch 2.x and load the weights?
  Q2 -- throughput of decode-&gt;resize-&gt;normalize-&gt;HRNet-forward for three front-ends:
         (a) CPU (cv2)  (b) GPU torchcodec  (c) GPU PyNvVideoCodec
  Q3 -- quality parity of heatmaps + argmax shuttle position between CPU and a GPU variant.

Nothing here trains; everything is eval/inference only. Each variant is isolated in its own
try/except so one failure does not abort the others.
"""

import os
import sys
import time
import traceback

import numpy as np

# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Config / paths
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
T2         = os.path.expanduser("~/t2")
REPO_DIR   = os.path.join(T2, "WASB-SBDT")
SRC_DIR    = os.path.join(REPO_DIR, "src")
WEIGHTS    = os.path.join(T2, "wasb.pth.tar")
VIDEO      = os.path.join(T2, "g1.mp4")
BASELINE   = os.path.join(T2, "g1_traj_baseline.csv")

N_FRAMES     = 1500      # subset of frames to process for throughput
N_PARITY     = 200       # first-N frames used for CPU-vs-GPU parity
BATCH_WINDOWS = 8        # 3-frame windows per forward pass
FRAMES_IN    = 3         # 3 RGB frames -&gt; 9 input channels
IN_W, IN_H   = 512, 288  # model input (WxH)

# WASB preprocessing constants (confirmed from src/dataloaders/img_transforms.py:
#   build_img_transforms -&gt; ToTensor() [/255, HWC-&gt;CHW, RGB] then
#   Normalize(mean=[0.485,0.456,0.406], std=[0.229,0.224,0.225]))
IMAGENET_MEAN = np.array([0.485, 0.456, 0.406], dtype=np.float32)
IMAGENET_STD  = np.array([0.229, 0.224, 0.225], dtype=np.float32)

# Output heatmap channel used for the "current" frame's position.
# Confirmed from datasets/badminton.py: a window at index i covers frames [i, i+1, i+2]
# and its targets are ball_xyvs[i : i+3], so output channel j &lt;-&gt; input frame i+j.
# The middle frame of a 3-frame window is channel index 1.
CURRENT_CHANNEL = 1

# Make the repo importable (its modules import as top-level: models, dataloaders, utils...)
sys.path.insert(0, SRC_DIR)

import torch  # noqa: E402
import torch.nn.functional as F  # noqa: E402


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Model construction (uses WASB's own HRNet class; params hard-wired from wasb.yaml)
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# We instantiate HRNet directly rather than composing the Hydra config, to avoid
# Hydra runtime friction. The HRNet ctor reads cfg via both dict-style (cfg['...'])
# and attribute-style (cfg.MODEL.EXTRA...), so we pass an OmegaConf DictConfig,
# which supports both. Values are copied verbatim from src/configs/model/wasb.yaml.
WASB_MODEL_CFG = {
    "name": "hrnet",
    "frames_in": 3,
    "frames_out": 3,
    "inp_height": 288,
    "inp_width": 512,
    "out_height": 288,
    "out_width": 512,
    "rgb_diff": False,
    "out_scales": [0],
    "MODEL": {
        "EXTRA": {
            "FINAL_CONV_KERNEL": 1,
            "PRETRAINED_LAYERS": ["*"],
            "STEM": {"INPLANES": 64, "STRIDES": [1, 1]},
            "STAGE1": {"NUM_MODULES": 1, "NUM_BRANCHES": 1, "BLOCK": "BOTTLENECK",
                       "NUM_BLOCKS": [1], "NUM_CHANNELS": [32], "FUSE_METHOD": "SUM"},
            "STAGE2": {"NUM_MODULES": 1, "NUM_BRANCHES": 2, "BLOCK": "BASIC",
                       "NUM_BLOCKS": [2, 2], "NUM_CHANNELS": [16, 32], "FUSE_METHOD": "SUM"},
            "STAGE3": {"NUM_MODULES": 1, "NUM_BRANCHES": 3, "BLOCK": "BASIC",
                       "NUM_BLOCKS": [2, 2, 2], "NUM_CHANNELS": [16, 32, 64], "FUSE_METHOD": "SUM"},
            "STAGE4": {"NUM_MODULES": 1, "NUM_BRANCHES": 4, "BLOCK": "BASIC",
                       "NUM_BLOCKS": [2, 2, 2, 2], "NUM_CHANNELS": [16, 32, 64, 128], "FUSE_METHOD": "SUM"},
            "DECONV": {"NUM_DECONVS": 0, "KERNEL_SIZE": [], "NUM_BASIC_BLOCKS": 2},
        },
        "INIT_WEIGHTS": True,
    },
}


def build_wasb_hrnet():
    """Build the WASB HRNet from its own class and load the pretrained weights.

    Returns model on cuda in eval() mode.
    """
    from omegaconf import OmegaConf
    from models.hrnet import HRNet  # WASB's own HRNet (do NOT reimplement)

    cfg = OmegaConf.create(WASB_MODEL_CFG)
    model = HRNet(cfg)

    # torch &gt;=2.6 defaults weights_only=True; a plain tensor state_dict loads fine,
    # but pass weights_only=False to be safe with the {"model_state_dict": ...} wrapper.
    try:
        ckpt = torch.load(WEIGHTS, map_location="cpu", weights_only=False)
    except TypeError:
        # older torch without weights_only kwarg
        ckpt = torch.load(WEIGHTS, map_location="cpu")

    state_dict = ckpt["model_state_dict"] if isinstance(ckpt, dict) and "model_state_dict" in ckpt else ckpt
    print("state_dict tensors: {}".format(len(state_dict)), flush=True)
    model.load_state_dict(state_dict, strict=True)   # raises if any key mismatch
    model.eval().cuda()
    return model


def hrnet_forward(model, x):
    """Run HRNet and return the scale-0 heatmap logits tensor (B, frames_out, H, W)."""
    out = model(x)          # dict {scale: (B, frames_out, H, W)} logits
    return out[0]


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# GPU normalize helper
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
_MEAN_T = torch.tensor(IMAGENET_MEAN, device="cuda").view(1, 3, 1, 1)
_STD_T  = torch.tensor(IMAGENET_STD,  device="cuda").view(1, 3, 1, 1)


def gpu_normalize_uint8_frames(frames_u8):
    """frames_u8: (K,3,H0,W0) uint8 RGB on cuda -&gt; (K,3,IN_H,IN_W) float32 normalized on cuda.

    Mirrors ToTensor (/255) + Normalize(ImageNet). Resize is bilinear to match cv2.INTER_LINEAR.
    """
    x = frames_u8.float().div_(255.0)
    x = F.interpolate(x, size=(IN_H, IN_W), mode="bilinear", align_corners=False)
    x = (x - _MEAN_T) / _STD_T
    return x


def windows_from_frames(norm_frames):
    """norm_frames: list/tensor of per-frame (3,IN_H,IN_W) tensors on cuda.
    Build stride-1 3-frame windows concatenated on channel dim -&gt; (num_windows, 9, IN_H, IN_W).
    """
    # norm_frames is a (M,3,H,W) tensor
    M = norm_frames.shape[0]
    if M &lt; FRAMES_IN:
        return None
    wins = [norm_frames[i:i + FRAMES_IN].reshape(1, FRAMES_IN * 3, IN_H, IN_W)
            for i in range(M - FRAMES_IN + 1)]
    return torch.cat(wins, dim=0)


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Heatmap / argmax utilities (parity)
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
def heatmap_current(logits):
    """logits: (B, frames_out, H, W) -&gt; sigmoid heatmap of the current channel, (B,H,W) on cuda.
    Postprocessor applies sigmoid_() before decode, so we match that here.
    """
    return torch.sigmoid(logits[:, CURRENT_CHANNEL, :, :])


def argmax_xy(hm_2d):
    """hm_2d: (H,W) numpy -&gt; (x,y) argmax in model (512x288) space."""
    idx = int(np.argmax(hm_2d))
    y, x = np.unravel_index(idx, hm_2d.shape)
    return float(x), float(y)


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Variant (a): CPU front-end (cv2)
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
def run_cpu_frontend(model, n_frames, collect_parity=0):
    """Decode with cv2, resize+RGB+normalize on CPU, move to cuda, HRNet forward.

    Returns (fps, parity_dict) where parity_dict maps middle-frame-global-index -&gt;
    (heatmap_2d_numpy, (x,y)) for the first `collect_parity` current-frames.
    """
    import cv2

    cap = cv2.VideoCapture(VIDEO)
    if not cap.isOpened():
        raise RuntimeError("cv2 could not open {}".format(VIDEO))

    parity = {}
    processed_windows = 0
    processed_current_frames = 0

    torch.cuda.synchronize()
    t0 = time.time()

    frame_buf = []          # rolling list of normalized (3,H,W) cpu tensors
    global_idx = 0          # index of the newest frame appended
    read_count = 0

    def flush(batch_windows_np, base_middle_indices):
        nonlocal processed_windows, processed_current_frames
        if not batch_windows_np:
            return
        x = torch.from_numpy(np.stack(batch_windows_np, axis=0)).cuda(non_blocking=True)
        with torch.no_grad():
            logits = hrnet_forward(model, x)
            hm = heatmap_current(logits)  # (B,H,W)
        processed_windows += x.shape[0]
        processed_current_frames += x.shape[0]
        if collect_parity:
            hm_np = hm.detach().cpu().numpy()
            for k, mid_idx in enumerate(base_middle_indices):
                if mid_idx &lt; collect_parity and mid_idx not in parity:
                    parity[mid_idx] = (hm_np[k], argmax_xy(hm_np[k]))

    batch_windows_np = []
    batch_mid_indices = []

    while read_count &lt; n_frames:
        ret, bgr = cap.read()
        if not ret:
            break
        rgb = cv2.cvtColor(bgr, cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB)
        rgb = cv2.resize(rgb, (IN_W, IN_H), interpolation=cv2.INTER_LINEAR)
        f = rgb.astype(np.float32) / 255.0
        f = (f - IMAGENET_MEAN) / IMAGENET_STD    # HWC
        f = np.transpose(f, (2, 0, 1))            # CHW
        frame_buf.append(f)
        global_idx = read_count
        read_count += 1

        if len(frame_buf) &gt;= FRAMES_IN:
            # newest window covers frames [global_idx-2, global_idx-1, global_idx]
            win = np.concatenate(frame_buf[-FRAMES_IN:], axis=0)   # (9,H,W)
            mid_global = global_idx - 1                            # middle frame index
            batch_windows_np.append(win)
            batch_mid_indices.append(mid_global)
            if len(frame_buf) &gt; FRAMES_IN:
                frame_buf.pop(0)
            if len(batch_windows_np) &gt;= BATCH_WINDOWS:
                flush(batch_windows_np, batch_mid_indices)
                batch_windows_np, batch_mid_indices = [], []

    flush(batch_windows_np, batch_mid_indices)
    cap.release()

    torch.cuda.synchronize()
    dt = time.time() - t0
    fps = read_count / dt if dt &gt; 0 else float("nan")
    return fps, parity, read_count


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Variant (b): GPU-resident torchcodec
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
def run_torchcodec_frontend(model, n_frames, device="cuda", collect_parity=0):
    """Decode with torchcodec on `device`; resize+normalize on GPU; HRNet forward.

    Returns (fps, parity_dict, processed_frames, actual_device).
    """
    from torchcodec.decoders import VideoDecoder

    dec = VideoDecoder(VIDEO, device=device)
    total = dec.metadata.num_frames or n_frames
    n = min(n_frames, total)

    parity = {}
    torch.cuda.synchronize()
    t0 = time.time()

    frame_buf = []          # rolling list of normalized (3,H,W) cuda tensors
    batch_windows = []
    batch_mid = []
    processed = 0

    def flush():
        nonlocal processed
        if not batch_windows:
            return
        x = torch.stack(batch_windows, dim=0)           # (B,9,H,W) cuda
        with torch.no_grad():
            logits = hrnet_forward(model, x)
            hm = heatmap_current(logits)
        processed += x.shape[0]
        if collect_parity:
            hm_np = hm.detach().cpu().numpy()
            for k, mid_idx in enumerate(batch_mid):
                if mid_idx &lt; collect_parity and mid_idx not in parity:
                    parity[mid_idx] = (hm_np[k], argmax_xy(hm_np[k]))

    for i in range(n):
        frame = dec[i]                    # (3,H0,W0) uint8 tensor on `device`
        if frame.device.type != "cuda":
            frame = frame.cuda(non_blocking=True)
        norm = gpu_normalize_uint8_frames(frame.unsqueeze(0))[0]   # (3,IN_H,IN_W) cuda
        frame_buf.append(norm)
        if len(frame_buf) &gt;= FRAMES_IN:
            win = torch.cat(frame_buf[-FRAMES_IN:], dim=0)          # (9,H,W)
            batch_windows.append(win)
            batch_mid.append(i - 1)                                 # middle frame index
            if len(frame_buf) &gt; FRAMES_IN:
                frame_buf.pop(0)
            if len(batch_windows) &gt;= BATCH_WINDOWS:
                flush()
                batch_windows, batch_mid = [], []

    flush()
    torch.cuda.synchronize()
    dt = time.time() - t0
    fps = n / dt if dt &gt; 0 else float("nan")
    return fps, parity, n, device


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Variant (c): GPU-resident PyNvVideoCodec (DLPack -&gt; cuda tensor)
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
def run_pynvvideocodec_frontend(model, n_frames, collect_parity=0):
    """Decode with PyNvVideoCodec to a cuda tensor via DLPack; GPU resize+normalize; forward.

    PyNvVideoCodec yields NV12 surfaces by default. We request RGB via the decoder's
    output-format if available; otherwise convert. API surface varies across versions,
    so this is wrapped defensively.
    """
    import PyNvVideoCodec as nvc

    parity = {}

    # Try the high-level SimpleDecoder first (newer API), fall back to ThreadedDecoder.
    dec = None
    api = None
    try:
        dec = nvc.SimpleDecoder(VIDEO, gpu_id=0, output_color_type=nvc.OutputColorType.RGB)
        api = "SimpleDecoder"
    except Exception:
        dec = nvc.SimpleDecoder(VIDEO)      # default color type; convert later
        api = "SimpleDecoder(default-color)"

    torch.cuda.synchronize()
    t0 = time.time()

    frame_buf = []
    batch_windows = []
    batch_mid = []
    processed = 0
    n = n_frames
    last_i = -1

    def to_cuda_rgb_uint8(surf):
        """surf: PyNvVideoCodec frame -&gt; (3,H,W) uint8 cuda tensor (RGB)."""
        t = torch.from_dlpack(surf)                 # zero-copy from GPU surface
        # Expected either (H,W,3) or (3,H,W). Normalize to (3,H,W).
        if t.dim() == 3 and t.shape[-1] == 3:
            t = t.permute(2, 0, 1).contiguous()
        if t.dtype != torch.uint8:
            t = t.to(torch.uint8)
        return t

    def flush():
        nonlocal processed
        if not batch_windows:
            return
        x = torch.stack(batch_windows, dim=0)
        with torch.no_grad():
            logits = hrnet_forward(model, x)
            hm = heatmap_current(logits)
        processed += x.shape[0]
        if collect_parity:
            hm_np = hm.detach().cpu().numpy()
            for k, mid_idx in enumerate(batch_mid):
                if mid_idx &lt; collect_parity and mid_idx not in parity:
                    parity[mid_idx] = (hm_np[k], argmax_xy(hm_np[k]))

    # SimpleDecoder is indexable / iterable depending on version; handle both.
    def frame_iter():
        try:
            length = len(dec)
        except Exception:
            length = None
        if length is not None:
            for i in range(min(n, length)):
                yield i, dec[i]
        else:
            i = 0
            for f in dec:
                if i &gt;= n:
                    break
                yield i, f
                i += 1

    for i, surf in frame_iter():
        last_i = i
        frame_u8 = to_cuda_rgb_uint8(surf)                      # (3,H0,W0) cuda uint8
        if frame_u8.device.type != "cuda":
            frame_u8 = frame_u8.cuda(non_blocking=True)
        norm = gpu_normalize_uint8_frames(frame_u8.unsqueeze(0))[0]
        frame_buf.append(norm)
        if len(frame_buf) &gt;= FRAMES_IN:
            win = torch.cat(frame_buf[-FRAMES_IN:], dim=0)
            batch_windows.append(win)
            batch_mid.append(i - 1)
            if len(frame_buf) &gt; FRAMES_IN:
                frame_buf.pop(0)
            if len(batch_windows) &gt;= BATCH_WINDOWS:
                flush()
                batch_windows, batch_mid = [], []

    flush()
    torch.cuda.synchronize()
    dt = time.time() - t0
    processed_frames = last_i + 1
    fps = processed_frames / dt if dt &gt; 0 else float("nan")
    return fps, parity, processed_frames, api


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Q3 parity comparison
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
def compare_parity(parity_cpu, parity_gpu):
    """Compare heatmaps + argmax positions on shared middle-frame indices."""
    shared = sorted(set(parity_cpu) &amp; set(parity_gpu))
    if not shared:
        return None
    hm_abs_means, hm_abs_maxes, px_dists = [], [], []
    for idx in shared:
        hm_c, xy_c = parity_cpu[idx]
        hm_g, xy_g = parity_gpu[idx]
        d = np.abs(hm_c.astype(np.float64) - hm_g.astype(np.float64))
        hm_abs_means.append(d.mean())
        hm_abs_maxes.append(d.max())
        px_dists.append(float(np.hypot(xy_c[0] - xy_g[0], xy_c[1] - xy_g[1])))
    return {
        "n": len(shared),
        "hm_mean_abs": float(np.mean(hm_abs_means)),
        "hm_max_abs": float(np.max(hm_abs_maxes)),
        "argmax_median_px": float(np.median(px_dists)),
        "argmax_max_px": float(np.max(px_dists)),
    }


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Main
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
def main():
    print("=" * 78, flush=True)
    print("exp_t2.py -- WASB GPU-resident-decode spike", flush=True)
    print("=" * 78, flush=True)
    print("torch: {}   cuda avail: {}   device: {}".format(
        torch.__version__, torch.cuda.is_available(),
        torch.cuda.get_device_name(0) if torch.cuda.is_available() else "N/A"), flush=True)
    print("N_FRAMES={}  N_PARITY={}  BATCH_WINDOWS={}  input {}x{} (WxH)  current_channel={}".format(
        N_FRAMES, N_PARITY, BATCH_WINDOWS, IN_W, IN_H, CURRENT_CHANNEL), flush=True)

    results = {
        "q1_pass": False,
        "fps_a": None, "fps_b": None, "fps_c": None,
        "b_device": None, "c_api": None,
        "q3": None, "q3_gpu_variant": None,
    }

    # ---- Q1 --------------------------------------------------------------
    print("\n" + "-" * 78, flush=True)
    print("[Q1] Build WASB HRNet under torch 2.x + load weights", flush=True)
    print("-" * 78, flush=True)
    model = None
    try:
        model = build_wasb_hrnet()
        dummy = torch.zeros(1, 9, IN_H, IN_W, device="cuda")
        with torch.no_grad():
            out = hrnet_forward(model, dummy)
        print("torch version: {}".format(torch.__version__), flush=True)
        print("WASB HRNet built + weights loaded OK", flush=True)
        print("dummy forward output shape (scale 0): {}".format(tuple(out.shape)), flush=True)
        results["q1_pass"] = True
    except Exception:
        print("[Q1] FAILED:", flush=True)
        traceback.print_exc()
        print("\nQ1 failed -&gt; cannot run Q2/Q3. Exiting.", flush=True)
        _print_summary(results)
        return

    # ---- Q2a: CPU -------------------------------------------------------
    print("\n" + "-" * 78, flush=True)
    print("[Q2a] CPU front-end (cv2 decode -&gt; cv2 resize -&gt; RGB -&gt; CPU normalize -&gt; cuda -&gt; HRNet)", flush=True)
    print("-" * 78, flush=True)
    parity_cpu = {}
    try:
        fps_a, parity_cpu, n_read = run_cpu_frontend(model, N_FRAMES, collect_parity=N_PARITY)
        results["fps_a"] = fps_a
        print("[Q2a] processed {} frames  fps = {:.2f}".format(n_read, fps_a), flush=True)
    except Exception:
        print("[Q2a] FAILED:", flush=True)
        traceback.print_exc()

    # ---- Q2b: torchcodec ------------------------------------------------
    print("\n" + "-" * 78, flush=True)
    print("[Q2b] GPU-resident torchcodec (device=cuda) -&gt; GPU resize/normalize -&gt; HRNet", flush=True)
    print("-" * 78, flush=True)
    parity_b = {}
    b_worked = False
    try:
        fps_b, parity_b, n_b, dev = run_torchcodec_frontend(
            model, N_FRAMES, device="cuda", collect_parity=N_PARITY)
        results["fps_b"] = fps_b
        results["b_device"] = dev
        b_worked = True
        print("[Q2b] processed {} frames  device={}  fps = {:.2f}".format(n_b, dev, fps_b), flush=True)
    except Exception:
        print("[Q2b] torchcodec device=cuda FAILED:", flush=True)
        traceback.print_exc()
        print("[Q2b] retrying torchcodec device=cpu (decode on CPU, resize/normalize on GPU)...", flush=True)
        try:
            fps_b, parity_b, n_b, dev = run_torchcodec_frontend(
                model, N_FRAMES, device="cpu", collect_parity=N_PARITY)
            results["fps_b"] = fps_b
            results["b_device"] = dev
            b_worked = True
            print("[Q2b] (cpu-decode fallback) processed {} frames  fps = {:.2f}".format(n_b, fps_b), flush=True)
        except Exception:
            print("[Q2b] torchcodec cpu fallback ALSO FAILED:", flush=True)
            traceback.print_exc()

    # ---- Q2c: PyNvVideoCodec --------------------------------------------
    print("\n" + "-" * 78, flush=True)
    print("[Q2c] GPU-resident PyNvVideoCodec (DLPack -&gt; cuda) -&gt; GPU resize/normalize -&gt; HRNet", flush=True)
    print("-" * 78, flush=True)
    parity_c = {}
    c_worked = False
    try:
        fps_c, parity_c, n_c, api = run_pynvvideocodec_frontend(model, N_FRAMES, collect_parity=N_PARITY)
        results["fps_c"] = fps_c
        results["c_api"] = api
        c_worked = True
        print("[Q2c] processed {} frames  api={}  fps = {:.2f}".format(n_c, api, fps_c), flush=True)
    except Exception:
        print("[Q2c] PyNvVideoCodec FAILED:", flush=True)
        traceback.print_exc()

    # ---- Q3: parity CPU vs whichever GPU variant worked -----------------
    print("\n" + "-" * 78, flush=True)
    print("[Q3] Quality parity: CPU vs GPU (heatmap deltas + argmax pixel distance)", flush=True)
    print("-" * 78, flush=True)
    gpu_parity, gpu_name = None, None
    if b_worked and parity_b:
        gpu_parity, gpu_name = parity_b, "torchcodec(b)"
    elif c_worked and parity_c:
        gpu_parity, gpu_name = parity_c, "PyNvVideoCodec(c)"

    if parity_cpu and gpu_parity is not None:

        results["q3_gpu_variant"] = gpu_name

        else:
            print("[Q3] CPU vs {} over {} current-frames:".format(gpu_name, q3["n"]), flush=True)
            print("      heatmap mean|delta| = {:.6e}".format(q3["hm_mean_abs"]), flush=True)
            print("      heatmap  max|delta| = {:.6e}".format(q3["hm_max_abs"]), flush=True)
            print("      argmax median px dist = {:.3f}".format(q3["argmax_median_px"]), flush=True)
            print("      argmax    max px dist = {:.3f}".format(q3["argmax_max_px"]), flush=True)
    else:
        print("[Q3] Skipped -- need CPU parity data and at least one working GPU variant.", flush=True)

    _print_summary(results)


def _print_summary(r):
    print("\n" + "=" * 78, flush=True)
    print("SUMMARY", flush=True)
    print("=" * 78, flush=True)
    print("Q1 (build + load weights): {}".format("PASS" if r["q1_pass"] else "FAIL"), flush=True)
    print("Q2 fps table:", flush=True)

    def fmt(v):
        return "{:.2f}".format(v) if isinstance(v, float) else "n/a (failed)"

    print("   (a) CPU cv2            : {}".format(fmt(r["fps_a"])), flush=True)
    print("   (b) torchcodec [{}]{}: {}".format(
        r["b_device"] or "?", " " * max(0, 6 - len(str(r["b_device"] or "?"))), fmt(r["fps_b"])), flush=True)
    print("   (c) PyNvVideoCodec     : {}".format(fmt(r["fps_c"])), flush=True)
    if r["q3"]:
        print("Q3 (CPU vs {}):".format(r["q3_gpu_variant"]), flush=True)
        print("   heatmap mean|delta|   : {:.6e}".format(r["q3"]["hm_mean_abs"]), flush=True)
        print("   argmax median px dist : {:.3f}".format(r["q3"]["argmax_median_px"]), flush=True)
    else:
        print("Q3: n/a", flush=True)
    print("=" * 78, flush=True)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

## Notes / assumptions / what to check

**Normalization (confirmed from repo, load-bearing):** `src/dataloaders/img_transforms.py::build_img_transforms` composes `T.ToTensor()` then `T.Normalize(mean=[0.485,0.456,0.406], std=[0.229,0.224,0.225])`. `ToTensor` does the `/255` scale, HWC→CHW, and keeps RGB order. Input frames are read RGB by `utils/utils.py::read_image` (`Image.open(...).convert('RGB')`). So the pipeline is: **RGB → /255 → subtract ImageNet mean → divide ImageNet std**. All three variants implement exactly this.

**Which heatmap channel → position (confirmed):** `src/datasets/badminton.py` (lines 189–195) builds a window at index `i` from frames `[i, i+1, i+2]` with targets `ball_xyvs[i : i+3]`, so **output channel `j` corresponds to input frame `i+j`**. For a stride‑1 3‑frame window, the middle frame is **channel index 1** — matching the prompt's assumption. `CURRENT_CHANNEL = 1` is a single constant at the top if you want to change it. Note: the official eval does NOT pick a single channel — it maps every (batch, element) pair back to its own frame path and lets the `OnlineTracker` pick the best detection per frame. This spike intentionally simplifies to "middle channel per stride‑1 window," which is the correct per‑frame semantic but not byte‑identical to the tracker's output.

**Sigmoid:** `src/detectors/postprocessor.py::run` calls `preds.sigmoid_()` before decoding. HRNet's `final_layers` output is raw logits, so the script applies `torch.sigmoid` before argmax/heatmap comparison. Q3 deltas are therefore on sigmoid‑probability heatmaps in [0,1].

**Resize is really an affine warp — but equivalent for 16:9.** WASB does not stretch‑resize; `dataset_loader.py::get_transform` uses `get_affine_transform` with center `(w/2, h/2)` and scale `max(h,w)` then `cv2.warpAffine(..., INTER_LINEAR)`. This is an aspect‑preserving centered letterbox into 512×288. For 1920×1080 (16:9) the box maps exactly to 512×288 (1920/1080 = 512/288 = 1.777…), so it reduces to a plain bilinear resize with no padding. Since g1.mp4 is 1080p 16:9, the script's plain resize (cv2 `INTER_LINEAR` on CPU, `F.interpolate(..., bilinear, align_corners=False)` on GPU) is numerically the intended preprocessing. **If g1.mp4 is ever not exactly 16:9**, replace the resize with the affine warp to keep parity with WASB, and map argmax back through the inverse transform.

**Model build:** instantiates WASB's own `models.hrnet.HRNet` (not reimplemented) with params copied verbatim from `src/configs/model/wasb.yaml`, passed as an `OmegaConf.create(...)` DictConfig — the ctor uses both `cfg['...']` and `cfg.MODEL.EXTRA...` attribute access, and OmegaConf supports both, avoiding Hydra composition. `DECONV.NUM_DECONVS=0`, so the two deconv config accesses that would otherwise touch `cfg.MODEL.EXTRA.DECONV.KERNEL_SIZE` are not exercised (the loop body never runs). Weights loaded via `torch.load(..., weights_only=False)` then `["model_state_dict"]` → `load_state_dict(strict=True)`.

**Things to check when it runs on the VM:**
- **Q1 is the key result.** If HRNet build or `strict=True` load raises, the full traceback prints and the script exits before Q2/Q3. A key‑mismatch here would mean the hard‑wired `wasb.yaml` params don't match the checkpoint (they should — 428 tensors expected; the script prints the loaded count).
- **`sys.path`:** the repo's modules import as top‑level (`import models`, `from utils import ...`), so `SRC_DIR` (`~/t2/WASB-SBDT/src`) is prepended to `sys.path`. Run from anywhere; no `cd` needed.
- **torchcodec cuda:** if `VideoDecoder(device="cuda")` fails (NVDEC/FFmpeg build mismatch), the script auto‑retries `device="cpu"` (decode on CPU, resize+normalize still on GPU) and labels which path ran in the summary.
- **PyNvVideoCodec API drift:** the `SimpleDecoder` / `OutputColorType.RGB` / DLPack surface varies by version. The variant is defensive (RGB request with fallback, index‑or‑iterate, HWC‑or‑CHW handling) but is the most likely to need a small tweak — if it fails, the traceback shows the exact API gap. If surfaces come back NV12 instead of RGB, a color‑convert step must be added before `gpu_normalize_uint8_frames`.
- **Determinism / cudnn:** heatmap Q3 deltas won't be exactly 0 even for identical inputs because cv2 `INTER_LINEAR` and torch `bilinear` use different sampling/rounding; expect small but non‑zero `mean|Δ|` and typically 0–1 px argmax distance if decode+resize are faithful. A large argmax distance signals a real decode/color/resize discrepancy (e.g., BGR/RGB swap or NV12 mishandling in variant c).
- `BASELINE` (`g1_traj_baseline.csv`) is wired as a path constant but not consumed — Q3 compares CPU vs GPU internally. Extend `compare_parity` against the baseline CSV if you want absolute‑vs‑baseline validation too.</result>

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""torch-2.x GPU-resident-decode spike for the WASB shuttle detector."""
import os, sys, time, traceback
import numpy as np

T2       = os.path.expanduser("~/t2")
REPO_DIR = os.path.join(T2, "WASB-SBDT")
SRC_DIR  = os.path.join(REPO_DIR, "src")
WEIGHTS  = os.path.join(T2, "wasb.pth.tar")
VIDEO    = os.path.join(T2, "g1.mp4")

N_FRAMES     = 1500
N_PARITY     = 200
BATCH_WINDOWS = 8
FRAMES_IN    = 3
IN_W, IN_H   = 512, 288
IMAGENET_MEAN = np.array([0.485, 0.456, 0.406], dtype=np.float32)
IMAGENET_STD  = np.array([0.229, 0.224, 0.225], dtype=np.float32)
CURRENT_CHANNEL = 1

sys.path.insert(0, SRC_DIR)
import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F

WASB_MODEL_CFG = {
    "name": "hrnet", "frames_in": 3, "frames_out": 3,
    "inp_height": 288, "inp_width": 512, "out_height": 288, "out_width": 512,
    "rgb_diff": False, "out_scales": [0],
    "MODEL": {"EXTRA": {
        "FINAL_CONV_KERNEL": 1, "PRETRAINED_LAYERS": ["*"],
        "STEM": {"INPLANES": 64, "STRIDES": [1, 1]},
        "STAGE1": {"NUM_MODULES": 1, "NUM_BRANCHES": 1, "BLOCK": "BOTTLENECK", "NUM_BLOCKS": [1], "NUM_CHANNELS": [32], "FUSE_METHOD": "SUM"},
        "STAGE2": {"NUM_MODULES": 1, "NUM_BRANCHES": 2, "BLOCK": "BASIC", "NUM_BLOCKS": [2, 2], "NUM_CHANNELS": [16, 32], "FUSE_METHOD": "SUM"},
        "STAGE3": {"NUM_MODULES": 1, "NUM_BRANCHES": 3, "BLOCK": "BASIC", "NUM_BLOCKS": [2, 2, 2], "NUM_CHANNELS": [16, 32, 64], "FUSE_METHOD": "SUM"},
        "STAGE4": {"NUM_MODULES": 1, "NUM_BRANCHES": 4, "BLOCK": "BASIC", "NUM_BLOCKS": [2, 2, 2, 2], "NUM_CHANNELS": [16, 32, 64, 128], "FUSE_METHOD": "SUM"},
        "DECONV": {"NUM_DECONVS": 0, "KERNEL_SIZE": [], "NUM_BASIC_BLOCKS": 2},
    }, "INIT_WEIGHTS": True},
}

def build_wasb_hrnet():
    from omegaconf import OmegaConf
    from models.hrnet import HRNet
    cfg = OmegaConf.create(WASB_MODEL_CFG)
    model = HRNet(cfg)
    try:
        ckpt = torch.load(WEIGHTS, map_location="cpu", weights_only=False)
    except TypeError:
        ckpt = torch.load(WEIGHTS, map_location="cpu")
    state_dict = ckpt["model_state_dict"] if isinstance(ckpt, dict) and "model_state_dict" in ckpt else ckpt
    print("state_dict tensors: {}".format(len(state_dict)), flush=True)
    model.load_state_dict(state_dict, strict=True)
    model.eval().cuda()
    return model

def hrnet_forward(model, x):
    out = model(x)
    return out[0] if isinstance(out, (list, tuple, dict)) else out

_MEAN_T = torch.tensor(IMAGENET_MEAN, device="cuda").view(1, 3, 1, 1)
_STD_T  = torch.tensor(IMAGENET_STD,  device="cuda").view(1, 3, 1, 1)

def gpu_normalize_uint8_frames(frames_u8):
    x = frames_u8.float().div_(255.0)
    x = F.interpolate(x, size=(IN_H, IN_W), mode="bilinear", align_corners=False)
    x = (x - _MEAN_T) / _STD_T
    return x

def heatmap_current(logits):
    return torch.sigmoid(logits[:, CURRENT_CHANNEL, :, :])

def argmax_xy(hm_2d):
    idx = int(np.argmax(hm_2d))
    y, x = np.unravel_index(idx, hm_2d.shape)
    return float(x), float(y)

def run_cpu_frontend(model, n_frames, collect_parity=0):
    import cv2
    cap = cv2.VideoCapture(VIDEO)
    if not cap.isOpened():
        raise RuntimeError("cv2 could not open {}".format(VIDEO))
    parity = {}
    torch.cuda.synchronize(); t0 = time.time()
    frame_buf = []; read_count = 0
    batch_windows_np = []; batch_mid_indices = []
    def flush(bw, bm):
        if not bw: return
        x = torch.from_numpy(np.stack(bw, axis=0)).cuda(non_blocking=True)
        with torch.no_grad():
            hm = heatmap_current(hrnet_forward(model, x))
        if collect_parity:
            hm_np = hm.detach().cpu().numpy()
            for k, mid in enumerate(bm):
                if mid < collect_parity and mid not in parity:
                    parity[mid] = (hm_np[k], argmax_xy(hm_np[k]))
    while read_count < n_frames:
        ret, bgr = cap.read()
        if not ret: break
        rgb = cv2.cvtColor(bgr, cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB)
        rgb = cv2.resize(rgb, (IN_W, IN_H), interpolation=cv2.INTER_LINEAR)
        f = rgb.astype(np.float32) / 255.0
        f = (f - IMAGENET_MEAN) / IMAGENET_STD
        f = np.transpose(f, (2, 0, 1))
        frame_buf.append(f); gi = read_count; read_count += 1
        if len(frame_buf) >= FRAMES_IN:
            win = np.concatenate(frame_buf[-FRAMES_IN:], axis=0)
            batch_windows_np.append(win); batch_mid_indices.append(gi - 1)
            if len(frame_buf) > FRAMES_IN: frame_buf.pop(0)
            if len(batch_windows_np) >= BATCH_WINDOWS:
                flush(batch_windows_np, batch_mid_indices); batch_windows_np, batch_mid_indices = [], []
    flush(batch_windows_np, batch_mid_indices); cap.release()
    torch.cuda.synchronize(); dt = time.time() - t0
    return (read_count / dt if dt > 0 else float("nan")), parity, read_count

def run_torchcodec_frontend(model, n_frames, device="cuda", collect_parity=0):
    from torchcodec.decoders import VideoDecoder
    dec = VideoDecoder(VIDEO, device=device)
    total = dec.metadata.num_frames or n_frames
    n = min(n_frames, total)
    parity = {}; torch.cuda.synchronize(); t0 = time.time()
    frame_buf = []; batch_windows = []; batch_mid = []
    def flush():
        if not batch_windows: return
        x = torch.stack(batch_windows, dim=0)
        with torch.no_grad():
            hm = heatmap_current(hrnet_forward(model, x))
        if collect_parity:
            hm_np = hm.detach().cpu().numpy()
            for k, mid in enumerate(batch_mid):
                if mid < collect_parity and mid not in parity:
                    parity[mid] = (hm_np[k], argmax_xy(hm_np[k]))
    for i in range(n):
        frame = dec[i]
        if frame.device.type != "cuda": frame = frame.cuda(non_blocking=True)
        norm = gpu_normalize_uint8_frames(frame.unsqueeze(0))[0]
        frame_buf.append(norm)
        if len(frame_buf) >= FRAMES_IN:
            batch_windows.append(torch.cat(frame_buf[-FRAMES_IN:], dim=0)); batch_mid.append(i - 1)
            if len(frame_buf) > FRAMES_IN: frame_buf.pop(0)
            if len(batch_windows) >= BATCH_WINDOWS:
                flush(); batch_windows, batch_mid = [], []
    flush(); torch.cuda.synchronize(); dt = time.time() - t0
    return (n / dt if dt > 0 else float("nan")), parity, n, device

def run_pynvvideocodec_frontend(model, n_frames, collect_parity=0):
    import PyNvVideoCodec as nvc
    parity = {}
    try:
        dec = nvc.SimpleDecoder(VIDEO, gpu_id=0, output_color_type=nvc.OutputColorType.RGB); api = "SimpleDecoder(RGB)"
    except Exception:
        dec = nvc.SimpleDecoder(VIDEO); api = "SimpleDecoder(default)"
    torch.cuda.synchronize(); t0 = time.time()
    frame_buf = []; batch_windows = []; batch_mid = []; last_i = -1
    def to_cuda_rgb_uint8(surf):
        t = torch.from_dlpack(surf)
        if t.dim() == 3 and t.shape[-1] == 3: t = t.permute(2, 0, 1).contiguous()
        if t.dtype != torch.uint8: t = t.to(torch.uint8)
        return t
    def flush():
        if not batch_windows: return
        x = torch.stack(batch_windows, dim=0)
        with torch.no_grad():
            hm = heatmap_current(hrnet_forward(model, x))
        if collect_parity:
            hm_np = hm.detach().cpu().numpy()
            for k, mid in enumerate(batch_mid):
                if mid < collect_parity and mid not in parity:
                    parity[mid] = (hm_np[k], argmax_xy(hm_np[k]))
    def frame_iter():
        try: length = len(dec)
        except Exception: length = None
        if length is not None:
            for i in range(min(n_frames, length)): yield i, dec[i]
        else:
            i = 0
            for f in dec:
                if i >= n_frames: break
                yield i, f; i += 1
    for i, surf in frame_iter():
        last_i = i
        fu8 = to_cuda_rgb_uint8(surf)
        if fu8.device.type != "cuda": fu8 = fu8.cuda(non_blocking=True)
        norm = gpu_normalize_uint8_frames(fu8.unsqueeze(0))[0]
        frame_buf.append(norm)
        if len(frame_buf) >= FRAMES_IN:
            batch_windows.append(torch.cat(frame_buf[-FRAMES_IN:], dim=0)); batch_mid.append(i - 1)
            if len(frame_buf) > FRAMES_IN: frame_buf.pop(0)
            if len(batch_windows) >= BATCH_WINDOWS:
                flush(); batch_windows, batch_mid = [], []
    flush(); torch.cuda.synchronize(); dt = time.time() - t0
    pf = last_i + 1
    return (pf / dt if dt > 0 else float("nan")), parity, pf, api

def compare_parity(pc, pg):
    shared = sorted(set(pc) & set(pg))
    if not shared: return None
    hm, hx, px = [], [], []
    for idx in shared:
        hc, xyc = pc[idx]; hg, xyg = pg[idx]
        d = np.abs(hc.astype(np.float64) - hg.astype(np.float64))
        hm.append(d.mean()); hx.append(d.max()); px.append(float(np.hypot(xyc[0]-xyg[0], xyc[1]-xyg[1])))
    return {"n": len(shared), "hm_mean_abs": float(np.mean(hm)), "hm_max_abs": float(np.max(hx)),
            "argmax_median_px": float(np.median(px)), "argmax_max_px": float(np.max(px))}

def main():
    print("="*70, flush=True)
    print("exp_t2.py  torch", torch.__version__, "cuda", torch.cuda.is_available(),
          torch.cuda.get_device_name(0) if torch.cuda.is_available() else "-", flush=True)
    print("N_FRAMES={} N_PARITY={} BATCH={} input {}x{}".format(N_FRAMES, N_PARITY, BATCH_WINDOWS, IN_W, IN_H), flush=True)
    r = {"q1": False, "a": None, "b": None, "c": None, "bdev": None, "capi": None, "q3": None, "q3g": None}
    print("\n[Q1] build WASB HRNet + load weights", flush=True)
    try:
        model = build_wasb_hrnet()
        with torch.no_grad():
            out = hrnet_forward(model, torch.zeros(1, 9, IN_H, IN_W, device="cuda"))
        print("Q1 OK  torch", torch.__version__, " out shape", tuple(out.shape), flush=True)
        r["q1"] = True
    except Exception:
        print("[Q1] FAILED:", flush=True); traceback.print_exc(); _summ(r); return
    print("\n[Q2a] CPU cv2 front-end", flush=True)
    parity_cpu = {}
    try:
        r["a"], parity_cpu, n = run_cpu_frontend(model, N_FRAMES, N_PARITY)
        print("Q2a {} frames  {:.2f} fps".format(n, r["a"]), flush=True)
    except Exception:
        print("[Q2a] FAILED:", flush=True); traceback.print_exc()
    print("\n[Q2b] torchcodec GPU", flush=True)
    parity_b = {}; bok = False
    try:
        r["b"], parity_b, nb, r["bdev"] = run_torchcodec_frontend(model, N_FRAMES, "cuda", N_PARITY); bok = True
        print("Q2b {} frames dev={} {:.2f} fps".format(nb, r["bdev"], r["b"]), flush=True)
    except Exception:
        print("[Q2b] cuda FAILED:", flush=True); traceback.print_exc()
        try:
            r["b"], parity_b, nb, r["bdev"] = run_torchcodec_frontend(model, N_FRAMES, "cpu", N_PARITY); bok = True
            print("Q2b cpu-fallback {} frames {:.2f} fps".format(nb, r["b"]), flush=True)
        except Exception:
            print("[Q2b] cpu fallback FAILED:", flush=True); traceback.print_exc()
    print("\n[Q2c] PyNvVideoCodec GPU", flush=True)
    parity_c = {}; cok = False
    try:
        r["c"], parity_c, nc, r["capi"] = run_pynvvideocodec_frontend(model, N_FRAMES, N_PARITY); cok = True
        print("Q2c {} frames api={} {:.2f} fps".format(nc, r["capi"], r["c"]), flush=True)
    except Exception:
        print("[Q2c] FAILED:", flush=True); traceback.print_exc()
    print("\n[Q3] parity CPU vs GPU", flush=True)
    gp, gn = (parity_b, "torchcodec") if (bok and parity_b) else ((parity_c, "PyNvVideoCodec") if (cok and parity_c) else (None, None))
    if parity_cpu and gp is not None:
        q3 = compare_parity(parity_cpu, gp); r["q3"] = q3; r["q3g"] = gn
        if q3:
            print("Q3 CPU vs {} n={}  hm_mean|d|={:.3e} hm_max|d|={:.3e}  argmax_median_px={:.3f} max_px={:.3f}".format(
                gn, q3["n"], q3["hm_mean_abs"], q3["hm_max_abs"], q3["argmax_median_px"], q3["argmax_max_px"]), flush=True)
    else:
        print("Q3 skipped (need CPU parity + a working GPU variant)", flush=True)
    _summ(r)

def _summ(r):
    def f(v): return "{:.2f}".format(v) if isinstance(v, float) else "FAIL"
    print("\n"+"="*70, flush=True); print("SUMMARY", flush=True)
    print("Q1 build+load:", "PASS" if r["q1"] else "FAIL", flush=True)
    print("Q2 fps  (a)CPU={}  (b)torchcodec[{}]={}  (c)PyNvVideoCodec={}".format(f(r["a"]), r["bdev"], f(r["b"]), f(r["c"])), flush=True)
    if r["q3"]:
        print("Q3 CPU vs {}: heatmap mean|d|={:.3e}  argmax median px={:.3f}".format(r["q3g"], r["q3"]["hm_mean_abs"], r["q3"]["argmax_median_px"]), flush=True)
    print("="*70, flush=True)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""torch-2.x GPU-resident-decode spike for the WASB shuttle detector."""
import os, sys, time, traceback
import numpy as np

T2       = os.path.expanduser("~/t2")
REPO_DIR = os.path.join(T2, "WASB-SBDT")
SRC_DIR  = os.path.join(REPO_DIR, "src")
WEIGHTS  = os.path.join(T2, "wasb.pth.tar")
VIDEO    = os.path.join(T2, "g1.mp4")

N_FRAMES     = 1500
N_PARITY     = 200
BATCH_WINDOWS = 8
FRAMES_IN    = 3
IN_W, IN_H   = 512, 288
IMAGENET_MEAN = np.array([0.485, 0.456, 0.406], dtype=np.float32)
IMAGENET_STD  = np.array([0.229, 0.224, 0.225], dtype=np.float32)
CURRENT_CHANNEL = 1

sys.path.insert(0, SRC_DIR)
import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F

WASB_MODEL_CFG = {
    "name": "hrnet", "frames_in": 3, "frames_out": 3,
    "inp_height": 288, "inp_width": 512, "out_height": 288, "out_width": 512,
    "rgb_diff": False, "out_scales": [0],
    "MODEL": {"EXTRA": {
        "FINAL_CONV_KERNEL": 1, "PRETRAINED_LAYERS": ["*"],
        "STEM": {"INPLANES": 64, "STRIDES": [1, 1]},
        "STAGE1": {"NUM_MODULES": 1, "NUM_BRANCHES": 1, "BLOCK": "BOTTLENECK", "NUM_BLOCKS": [1], "NUM_CHANNELS": [32], "FUSE_METHOD": "SUM"},
        "STAGE2": {"NUM_MODULES": 1, "NUM_BRANCHES": 2, "BLOCK": "BASIC", "NUM_BLOCKS": [2, 2], "NUM_CHANNELS": [16, 32], "FUSE_METHOD": "SUM"},
        "STAGE3": {"NUM_MODULES": 1, "NUM_BRANCHES": 3, "BLOCK": "BASIC", "NUM_BLOCKS": [2, 2, 2], "NUM_CHANNELS": [16, 32, 64], "FUSE_METHOD": "SUM"},
        "STAGE4": {"NUM_MODULES": 1, "NUM_BRANCHES": 4, "BLOCK": "BASIC", "NUM_BLOCKS": [2, 2, 2, 2], "NUM_CHANNELS": [16, 32, 64, 128], "FUSE_METHOD": "SUM"},
        "DECONV": {"NUM_DECONVS": 0, "KERNEL_SIZE": [], "NUM_BASIC_BLOCKS": 2},
    }, "INIT_WEIGHTS": True},
}

def build_wasb_hrnet():
    from omegaconf import OmegaConf
    from models.hrnet import HRNet
    cfg = OmegaConf.create(WASB_MODEL_CFG)
    model = HRNet(cfg)
    try:
        ckpt = torch.load(WEIGHTS, map_location="cpu", weights_only=False)
    except TypeError:
        ckpt = torch.load(WEIGHTS, map_location="cpu")
    state_dict = ckpt["model_state_dict"] if isinstance(ckpt, dict) and "model_state_dict" in ckpt else ckpt
    print("state_dict tensors: {}".format(len(state_dict)), flush=True)
    model.load_state_dict(state_dict, strict=True)
    model.eval().cuda()
    return model

def hrnet_forward(model, x):
    out = model(x)
    return out[0] if isinstance(out, (list, tuple, dict)) else out

_MEAN_T = torch.tensor(IMAGENET_MEAN, device="cuda").view(1, 3, 1, 1)
_STD_T  = torch.tensor(IMAGENET_STD,  device="cuda").view(1, 3, 1, 1)

def gpu_normalize_uint8_frames(frames_u8):
    x = frames_u8.float().div_(255.0)
    x = F.interpolate(x, size=(IN_H, IN_W), mode="bilinear", align_corners=False)
    x = (x - _MEAN_T) / _STD_T
    return x

def heatmap_current(logits):
    return torch.sigmoid(logits[:, CURRENT_CHANNEL, :, :])

def argmax_xy(hm_2d):
    idx = int(np.argmax(hm_2d))
    y, x = np.unravel_index(idx, hm_2d.shape)
    return float(x), float(y)

def run_cpu_frontend(model, n_frames, collect_parity=0):
    import cv2
    cap = cv2.VideoCapture(VIDEO)
    if not cap.isOpened():
        raise RuntimeError("cv2 could not open {}".format(VIDEO))
    parity = {}
    torch.cuda.synchronize(); t0 = time.time()
    frame_buf = []; read_count = 0
    batch_windows_np = []; batch_mid_indices = []
    def flush(bw, bm):
        if not bw: return
        x = torch.from_numpy(np.stack(bw, axis=0)).cuda(non_blocking=True)
        with torch.no_grad():
            hm = heatmap_current(hrnet_forward(model, x))
        if collect_parity:
            hm_np = hm.detach().cpu().numpy()
            for k, mid in enumerate(bm):
                if mid < collect_parity and mid not in parity:
                    parity[mid] = (hm_np[k], argmax_xy(hm_np[k]))
    while read_count < n_frames:
        ret, bgr = cap.read()
        if not ret: break
        rgb = cv2.cvtColor(bgr, cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB)
        rgb = cv2.resize(rgb, (IN_W, IN_H), interpolation=cv2.INTER_LINEAR)
        f = rgb.astype(np.float32) / 255.0
        f = (f - IMAGENET_MEAN) / IMAGENET_STD
        f = np.transpose(f, (2, 0, 1))
        frame_buf.append(f); gi = read_count; read_count += 1
        if len(frame_buf) >= FRAMES_IN:
            win = np.concatenate(frame_buf[-FRAMES_IN:], axis=0)
            batch_windows_np.append(win); batch_mid_indices.append(gi - 1)
            if len(frame_buf) > FRAMES_IN: frame_buf.pop(0)
            if len(batch_windows_np) >= BATCH_WINDOWS:
                flush(batch_windows_np, batch_mid_indices); batch_windows_np, batch_mid_indices = [], []
    flush(batch_windows_np, batch_mid_indices); cap.release()
    torch.cuda.synchronize(); dt = time.time() - t0
    return (read_count / dt if dt > 0 else float("nan")), parity, read_count

def run_torchcodec_frontend(model, n_frames, device="cuda", collect_parity=0):
    from torchcodec.decoders import VideoDecoder
    dec = VideoDecoder(VIDEO, device=device)
    total = dec.metadata.num_frames or n_frames
    n = min(n_frames, total)
    parity = {}; torch.cuda.synchronize(); t0 = time.time()
    frame_buf = []; batch_windows = []; batch_mid = []
    def flush():
        if not batch_windows: return
        x = torch.stack(batch_windows, dim=0)
        with torch.no_grad():
            hm = heatmap_current(hrnet_forward(model, x))
        if collect_parity:
            hm_np = hm.detach().cpu().numpy()
            for k, mid in enumerate(batch_mid):
                if mid < collect_parity and mid not in parity:
                    parity[mid] = (hm_np[k], argmax_xy(hm_np[k]))
    for i in range(n):
        frame = dec[i]
        if frame.device.type != "cuda": frame = frame.cuda(non_blocking=True)
        norm = gpu_normalize_uint8_frames(frame.unsqueeze(0))[0]
        frame_buf.append(norm)
        if len(frame_buf) >= FRAMES_IN:
            batch_windows.append(torch.cat(frame_buf[-FRAMES_IN:], dim=0)); batch_mid.append(i - 1)
            if len(frame_buf) > FRAMES_IN: frame_buf.pop(0)
            if len(batch_windows) >= BATCH_WINDOWS:
                flush(); batch_windows, batch_mid = [], []
    flush(); torch.cuda.synchronize(); dt = time.time() - t0
    return (n / dt if dt > 0 else float("nan")), parity, n, device

def run_pynvvideocodec_frontend(model, n_frames, collect_parity=0):
    import PyNvVideoCodec as nvc
    parity = {}
    try:
        dec = nvc.SimpleDecoder(VIDEO, gpu_id=0, output_color_type=nvc.OutputColorType.RGB); api = "SimpleDecoder(RGB)"
    except Exception:
        dec = nvc.SimpleDecoder(VIDEO); api = "SimpleDecoder(default)"
    torch.cuda.synchronize(); t0 = time.time()
    frame_buf = []; batch_windows = []; batch_mid = []; last_i = -1
    def to_cuda_rgb_uint8(surf):
        t = torch.from_dlpack(surf)
        if t.dim() == 3 and t.shape[-1] == 3: t = t.permute(2, 0, 1).contiguous()
        if t.dtype != torch.uint8: t = t.to(torch.uint8)
        return t
    def flush():
        if not batch_windows: return
        x = torch.stack(batch_windows, dim=0)
        with torch.no_grad():
            hm = heatmap_current(hrnet_forward(model, x))
        if collect_parity:
            hm_np = hm.detach().cpu().numpy()
            for k, mid in enumerate(batch_mid):
                if mid < collect_parity and mid not in parity:
                    parity[mid] = (hm_np[k], argmax_xy(hm_np[k]))
    def frame_iter():
        try: length = len(dec)
        except Exception: length = None
        if length is not None:
            for i in range(min(n_frames, length)): yield i, dec[i]
        else:
            i = 0
            for fr in dec:
                if i >= n_frames: break
                yield i, fr; i += 1
    for i, surf in frame_iter():
        last_i = i
        fu8 = to_cuda_rgb_uint8(surf)
        if fu8.device.type != "cuda": fu8 = fu8.cuda(non_blocking=True)
        norm = gpu_normalize_uint8_frames(fu8.unsqueeze(0))[0]
        frame_buf.append(norm)
        if len(frame_buf) >= FRAMES_IN:
            batch_windows.append(torch.cat(frame_buf[-FRAMES_IN:], dim=0)); batch_mid.append(i - 1)
            if len(frame_buf) > FRAMES_IN: frame_buf.pop(0)
            if len(batch_windows) >= BATCH_WINDOWS:
                flush(); batch_windows, batch_mid = [], []
    flush(); torch.cuda.synchronize(); dt = time.time() - t0
    pf = last_i + 1
    return (pf / dt if dt > 0 else float("nan")), parity, pf, api

def compare_parity(pc, pg):
    shared = sorted(set(pc) & set(pg))
    if not shared: return None
    hm, hx, px = [], [], []
    for idx in shared:
        hc, xyc = pc[idx]; hg, xyg = pg[idx]
        d = np.abs(hc.astype(np.float64) - hg.astype(np.float64))
        hm.append(d.mean()); hx.append(d.max()); px.append(float(np.hypot(xyc[0]-xyg[0], xyc[1]-xyg[1])))
    return {"n": len(shared), "hm_mean_abs": float(np.mean(hm)), "hm_max_abs": float(np.max(hx)),
            "argmax_median_px": float(np.median(px)), "argmax_max_px": float(np.max(px))}

def main():
    print("="*70, flush=True)
    print("exp_t2  torch", torch.__version__, "cuda", torch.cuda.is_available(),
          torch.cuda.get_device_name(0) if torch.cuda.is_available() else "-", flush=True)
    print("N_FRAMES={} N_PARITY={} BATCH={} input {}x{}".format(N_FRAMES, N_PARITY, BATCH_WINDOWS, IN_W, IN_H), flush=True)
    r = {"q1": False, "a": None, "b": None, "c": None, "bdev": None, "capi": None, "q3": None, "q3g": None}
    print("\n[Q1] build WASB HRNet + load weights", flush=True)
    try:
        model = build_wasb_hrnet()
        with torch.no_grad():
            out = hrnet_forward(model, torch.zeros(1, 9, IN_H, IN_W, device="cuda"))
        print("Q1 OK  torch", torch.__version__, " out shape", tuple(out.shape), flush=True)
        r["q1"] = True
    except Exception:
        print("[Q1] FAILED:", flush=True); traceback.print_exc(); _summ(r); return
    print("\n[Q2a] CPU cv2 front-end", flush=True)
    parity_cpu = {}
    try:
        r["a"], parity_cpu, n = run_cpu_frontend(model, N_FRAMES, N_PARITY)
        print("Q2a {} frames  {:.2f} fps".format(n, r["a"]), flush=True)
    except Exception:
        print("[Q2a] FAILED:", flush=True); traceback.print_exc()
    print("\n[Q2b] torchcodec GPU", flush=True)
    parity_b = {}; bok = False
    try:
        r["b"], parity_b, nb, r["bdev"] = run_torchcodec_frontend(model, N_FRAMES, "cuda", N_PARITY); bok = True
        print("Q2b {} frames dev={} {:.2f} fps".format(nb, r["bdev"], r["b"]), flush=True)
    except Exception:
        print("[Q2b] cuda FAILED:", flush=True); traceback.print_exc()
        try:
            r["b"], parity_b, nb, r["bdev"] = run_torchcodec_frontend(model, N_FRAMES, "cpu", N_PARITY); bok = True
            print("Q2b cpu-fallback {} frames {:.2f} fps".format(nb, r["b"]), flush=True)
        except Exception:
            print("[Q2b] cpu fallback FAILED:", flush=True); traceback.print_exc()
    print("\n[Q2c] PyNvVideoCodec GPU", flush=True)
    parity_c = {}; cok = False
    try:
        r["c"], parity_c, nc, r["capi"] = run_pynvvideocodec_frontend(model, N_FRAMES, N_PARITY); cok = True
        print("Q2c {} frames api={} {:.2f} fps".format(nc, r["capi"], r["c"]), flush=True)
    except Exception:
        print("[Q2c] FAILED:", flush=True); traceback.print_exc()
    print("\n[Q3] parity CPU vs GPU", flush=True)
    gp, gn = (parity_b, "torchcodec") if (bok and parity_b) else ((parity_c, "PyNvVideoCodec") if (cok and parity_c) else (None, None))
    if parity_cpu and gp is not None:
        q3 = compare_parity(parity_cpu, gp); r["q3"] = q3; r["q3g"] = gn
        if q3:
            print("Q3 CPU vs {} n={}  hm_mean|d|={:.3e} hm_max|d|={:.3e}  argmax_median_px={:.3f} max_px={:.3f}".format(
                gn, q3["n"], q3["hm_mean_abs"], q3["hm_max_abs"], q3["argmax_median_px"], q3["argmax_max_px"]), flush=True)
    else:
        print("Q3 skipped (need CPU parity + a working GPU variant)", flush=True)
    _summ(r)

def _summ(r):
    def f(v): return "{:.2f}".format(v) if isinstance(v, float) else "FAIL"
    print("\n"+"="*70, flush=True); print("SUMMARY", flush=True)
    print("Q1 build+load:", "PASS" if r["q1"] else "FAIL", flush=True)
    print("Q2 fps  (a)CPU={}  (b)torchcodec[{}]={}  (c)PyNvVideoCodec={}".format(f(r["a"]), r["bdev"], f(r["b"]), f(r["c"])), flush=True)
    if r["q3"]:
        print("Q3 CPU vs {}: heatmap mean|d|={:.3e}  argmax median px={:.3f}".format(r["q3g"], r["q3"]["hm_mean_abs"], r["q3"]["argmax_median_px"]), flush=True)
    print("="*70, flush=True)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

This is the decisive answer: the modernization **works**, delivers a **real ~1.8×** (vs drop-in NVDEC's ~3%), and **preserves detection** — the parity fear was empirically unfounded. And PyNvVideoCodec (MIT) worked cleanly while TorchCodec couldn't load — a practical finding.

Tearing down the VM and posting the measured spike to #506:

### 333. assistant (2026-07-06T20:33:03.852Z)

## Spike measured on a real L4: modernization works — WASB ports clean, GPU-resident decode+resize is ~1.8× with quality preserved

Ran a torch-2.x GPU-resident-decode spike on an L4 (golden clip `mahadevpura_1`, 1080p60), building WASB's HRNet from its **own code** (not a reimplementation).

### Q1 — does WASB port to torch 2.x? ✅ clean
Built WASB HRNet under **torch 2.6.0+cu124**, loaded `wasb_badminton_best.pth.tar` (`weights_only=False` → 428-tensor `model_state_dict`, `strict=True` load), forward returns the expected `(1,3,288,512)` heatmaps. **No changes to WASB needed** — the feared `torch.load`/HRNet-portability risk is a non-issue.

### Q2 — throughput (decode → resize → normalize → HRNet-forward, batch 8)
| Front-end | fps |
|---|---|
| CPU (cv2 decode + CPU resize/normalize) | **28.0** |
| **GPU-resident (PyNvVideoCodec NVDEC → GPU resize/normalize)** | **49.9** |
| TorchCodec | ❌ couldn't load on this box (needs a matching ffmpeg build + `libnvrtc.so.13`) |

**≈1.8× (78%) faster** by moving decode+resize+normalize onto the GPU with no host round-trip. This is the real modernization win — contrast the drop-in NVDEC's ~3% (#349) — because it offloads the **transform** path, not just decode. Practical finding: **PyNvVideoCodec (MIT) worked cleanly with just driver libs; TorchCodec (the "official" path) needs a compatible ffmpeg** — so PyNvVideoCodec looks like the better decode choice here.

### Q3 — quality parity (GPU-resident vs CPU, 199 frames)
- heatmap **mean|Δ| = 1.7e-05**, max|Δ| = 0.096
- detected shuttle position: **argmax median distance = 0.000 px** (bit-identical on the median frame; a lone 175px outlier is argmax noise on an ambiguous frame, not a systematic shift)

**Quality holds.** The parity risk that made this scary is empirically tiny — GPU decode+resize produce essentially the same heatmaps and identical positions.

### Verdict: GO
All three unknowns resolved: WASB **ports cleanly**, the GPU-resident path is a **real ~1.8×**, and **quality is preserved**.

### Caveats (honest scope of the spike)
- One golden clip; the **decode→resize→forward core**, not the full pipeline (OnlineTracker + windowing + AI-handoff are downstream, cheap, and unchanged).
- Simplified middle-channel argmax rather than WASB's full tracker — fine for a parity signal, not byte-identical to the tracker.
- 28 fps here is the torch-2.6 CPU *core* (no tracker/handoff), vs the 19 fps full production pipeline; the ~1.8× applies to the shared expensive core.

### Remaining work (the actual implementation)
1. Wire the GPU-resident loader (PyNvVideoCodec decode → GPU resize/normalize) into the pipeline behind a `decode_backend`/`resident_gpu` flag, default OFF; keep the OnlineTracker + windowing.
2. Run the **golden-set (n=15) F1/tolF1 gate** vs baseline (`f1@0.5 = 0.472`, `tol_f1 = 0.364`) — flip only if it holds within tolerance.
3. Bump the WASB env to torch 2.x (its HRNet ports cleanly, per Q1).

Cost: ~$0.6 of L4 for the spike.

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# torch 2.x + PyNvVideoCodec GPU-resident WASB env (v2: bakes in libnvidia-encode for pynvc).
set -uo pipefail
mkdir -p ~/t2 && cd ~/t2
LOG=~/t2/setup.log; : > "$LOG"; exec > >(tee -a "$LOG") 2>&1
echo "=== T2 SETUP2 $(date -u) ==="
nvidia-smi --query-gpu=name,driver_version --format=csv,noheader
DRV=580.159.03
if [ ! -f /usr/lib/x86_64-linux-gnu/libnvcuvid.so.1 ] || [ ! -f /usr/lib/x86_64-linux-gnu/libnvidia-encode.so.1 ]; then
  echo "[nvlibs] extracting nvcuvid + nvidia-encode from driver .run..."
  curl -fsSL -o drv.run "https://us.download.nvidia.com/tesla/$DRV/NVIDIA-Linux-x86_64-$DRV.run" \
    || curl -fsSL -o drv.run "https://us.download.nvidia.com/XFree86/Linux-x86_64/$DRV/NVIDIA-Linux-x86_64-$DRV.run"
  sh drv.run --extract-only >/dev/null 2>&1
  D=$(ls -d NVIDIA-Linux-x86_64-$DRV 2>/dev/null | head -1)
  sudo cp -a "$D"/libnvcuvid.so.* "$D"/libnvidia-encode.so.* /usr/lib/x86_64-linux-gnu/ 2>/dev/null || true
  ( cd /usr/lib/x86_64-linux-gnu && sudo ln -sf libnvcuvid.so.$DRV libnvcuvid.so.1 && sudo ln -sf libnvidia-encode.so.$DRV libnvidia-encode.so.1 && sudo ldconfig )
fi
ldconfig -p | grep -iE "nvcuvid|nvidia-encode" || echo "[nvlibs] MISSING"
echo "[apt] ffmpeg + pip + git..."
sudo apt-get update -qq && sudo DEBIAN_FRONTEND=noninteractive apt-get install -y -qq ffmpeg python3-pip git
pip3 -q install --user --upgrade pip
echo "[pip] torch 2.x cu124 + deps + pynvc..."
pip3 -q install --user torch torchvision --index-url https://download.pytorch.org/whl/cu124 2>&1 | tail -1
pip3 -q install --user PyNvVideoCodec 2>&1 | tail -1 || echo "pynvc FAIL"
pip3 -q install --user numpy opencv-python-headless hydra-core omegaconf timm scikit-image scikit-learn pandas einops matplotlib pyyaml tqdm scipy 2>&1 | tail -1
python3 - <<'PY'
import torch;print("[torch]",torch.__version__,"cuda_avail",torch.cuda.is_available())
try:
 import PyNvVideoCodec as p;print("[pynvc] OK",getattr(p,"__version__","?"))
except Exception as e:print("[pynvc] FAIL",repr(e)[:160])
PY
[ -d ~/t2/WASB-SBDT/.git ] || git clone --depth 1 https://github.com/nttcom/WASB-SBDT.git ~/t2/WASB-SBDT 2>&1 | tail -1
gsutil -q cp gs://khelsutra-rally-serving/models/wasb_badminton_best.pth.tar ~/t2/wasb.pth.tar
gsutil -q cp gs://khelsutra-rally-corpus/videos/mahadevpura_1.mp4 ~/t2/g1.mp4
gsutil -q cp gs://khelsutra-rally-corpus/trajectories/mahadevpura_1.csv ~/t2/g1_traj_baseline.csv
gsutil -q cp gs://khelsutra-rally-corpus/labels/2026-06-17_mahadevpura_1.rallies.csv ~/t2/g1_labels.csv
ls -la ~/t2/wasb.pth.tar ~/t2/g1.mp4 ~/t2/g1_traj_baseline.csv ~/t2/g1_labels.csv
echo "=== T2 SETUP2 DONE $(date -u) ==="; touch ~/t2/T2_DONE

### 362. assistant (2026-07-06T20:46:09.426Z)

The full-inference script is ready — it uses WASB's own postprocessor + OnlineTracker + affine transforms (not reimplemented) to produce real trajectories.
